NEC uPD6383GF host uploads (uC-IF capture)
I-RAM capacity is 384 words of 36 bits; a program may use FEWER.
5 bytes = one 36-bit I-RAM word, 3 bytes = one 24-bit C-RAM/D-RAM word (confirmed:
the KN5000 Sub CPU bytecode handlers divide by literal 5 and 3, and captured uploads
tile I-RAM exactly). Command 0x01 payloads are a 16-bit word address + N*5 bytes.
